heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v0.9.81 Stable2026-11-22T14:37:14Z

HyperFrames v0.9.81

Released on 2026-11-22.

A heavy page now renders without running out of memory: a render starts only as many browsers as fit. Sealed previews take more edits in place, from moves and crops to undo and hotkeys.

Fixes

  • Studio: Studio acts on a sealed preview's key report only after a real press or click (3ac5265aac, #8503).
  • Core: A preview of hundreds of clips with no set length no longer loads them all (d4e8d6f983, #8303).
  • Studio: Scrubbing across a cut shows the newest frame, not black (0e6879b8ef, #8501).
  • Studio: Copy and duplicate refuse a file that is not one of the project's compositions (45674e840f, #8490).
  • Studio: A sealed preview's clips keep their GSAP transition label (97b90d8fa2, #8499).
  • Studio: A sealed press held across a frame swap starts on the frame shown (a770f4b85d, #8492).
  • Studio: Paste and duplicate write only allowed markup unless it comes from the same file (0b73069201, #8483).
  • Studio: Rotating a layer in a sealed preview turns it from the angle its stylesheet shows (039eca4f0f, #8469).
  • Studio: A menu that does not fit below opens above, never over what opened it (679a5a7e0b, #8406).
  • Studio Server: A cut names its new half from the file's own id (fccd00e719, #8481).
  • Ci: A shard whose results never uploaded is re-run, and never read as an edit accuracy drop (71588b0a73, #8477).
  • Studio: A pasted element lands after its own original, never inside a script (bfd6cb755f, #8489).
  • Core: A preview seek waits for each graded video to draw its new frame (104eab37ad, #8488).
  • Core: A far clip the runtime lets go of keeps counting in the film's length (5ae70826ed, #8480).
  • Player: A sealed film no longer shows failed to load while its runtime starts (835e660e0e, #8472).
  • Studio: A sealed preview shows paused video frames from Studio's decoder (61008a40dd, #8466).
  • Studio: A drag behind a refused edit is refused only when it would land on a clip (c99cbbe44d, #8456).
  • Studio: A sealed Layers pick of a cropped layer puts its resize corners on the whole layer (f57552da49, #8468).
  • Ci: A rerun of the captures check reads the PR's current body (8f7e59742f, #8479).
  • Studio: A sealed move or resize ends its draft in the save's own frame message (7e26a6d3c0, #8473).
  • Core: Load the motion path plugin only for a page that tweens along a path (4157992664, #8449).
  • Studio: A thrown value whose name getter throws still falls back to a reload (f51e57b26e, #8474).
  • Engine: A heavy page starts only as many capture browsers as the machine's memory holds (969eeba6ee, #8452).
  • Studio: A timeline edit queued before a project switch stays in its own project (3e101f9654, #8458).
  • Studio: A sealed crop draws a layer's insets at its own scale and follows a seek (05d1bc8b1c, #8451).
  • Studio: An in-place tween patch that throws says why it fell back to a reload (e98ca56c4a, #8454).
  • Studio: A sealed resize keeps a self-centring layer where it is (926e1b5601, #8467).
  • Studio: An undo or redo that adds a sealed page's first edit stays in place (2d72e523ff, #8462).
  • Studio: Hiding an element hides it in the preview, sealed or not (d37563e35b, #8461).
  • Studio: A ripple shift queued behind a refused edit is refused before it writes (56e0d6fbd7, #8443).
  • Studio: A reloaded preview shows the saved grade, not one an undo took back (5e6068e94a, #8455).
  • Studio: A sealed canvas pick selects the timeline clip it belongs to (ff01d81be1, #8459).
  • Studio: A key pressed in a sealed preview reaches Studio's hotkeys (34d5c4678d, #8444).
  • CLI: A crashed or encoder-failed render tells Desktop its code (678fda62cd, #8434).
  • Studio: A preview clip's track is taken only as a whole number (bf21bf9f78, #8447).
  • Studio: A colour grade made in the property panel saves to the file (77f3b55fd3, #8450).
  • Studio: Moving and nudging an element works in a sealed preview (eea73b3107, #8263).
  • Share: Names print as plain text in the terminal, and a saved owner address must be a real address (d09a1809c1, #8341).
  • Studio: Timeline clips from the preview keep numeric fields, and drops write them as numbers (59ea053f10, #8438).
  • Studio: Caption edits save through the project's file writer (0774d96462, #8445).
  • Studio: A preview clip's track, start and length enter the timeline only as numbers (e1845e5dec, #8446).
  • Studio: The topology lens shows in a sealed preview (79e43a33a8, #8420).
  • Media Use: A manifest path naming a share or another root is refused without looking at it (2a24357338, #8437).
  • Studio: A refused delete also refuses the ripple shifts queued behind it (925cea1f1f, #8421).
  • Studio: A reload that gives up says whether its frame was missing, blank or unreadable (8ad7cbae24, #8419).
  • Studio: Show a sealed composition's clip on the timeline (28502224a5, #8408).
  • Media Use: Refuse links under .media before reserving, writing or recording (0495a57f76, #8327).

Docs & Examples

  • README: Lead with what HyperFrames makes, keep every developer section (84ff7d71c2, #8442).

Internal

  • CLI: Run the PowerShell round-trip names in one shell so slow Windows runners stay in time (680c9a5402, #8498).
  • CLI: The doctor output test no longer runs every machine check (aa262c244d, #8497).
  • Studio: The preview regression runs green against a sealed preview (3a68f7b3ca, #8470).
  • Studio: The edit-accuracy bench reads what a person ends with after sealed saves and undos (bc3d7b43e8, #8475).
  • Studio: The preview regression counts decoded covers, not the macOS gamma filter (154842851a, #8484).
  • Windows: Run the engine and CLI tests in three shards, not two, to stay under the step deadline (a638c31aa1, #8476).
  • Studio: A preview clip's number and track rules live in one place (deb478a3f6, #8471).
  • Studio: Chrome-driven tests run alone after the unit tests (c731046c77, #8440).
  • A studio change with no visible effect can say so at any size (a0b76d20fb, #8439).
  • CLI: A missed late SVG image in snapshot says which wait came up short (826b4fcc69, #8435).

v0.9.80 Stable2026-11-22T02:16:32Z

HyperFrames v0.9.80

Released on 2026-11-22.

Dropping a clip onto main-track clips can now insert it there. Studio previews match the export more closely, and a drive that stops answering no longer stalls a project.

Features

  • Studio: Dropping a clip onto main-track clips can insert it there (08bd7fc12, #8397).

Fixes

  • Engine: An asset download fails once when the egress proxy drops its tunnel (da1476e48, #8417).
  • Studio: An insert on drop never lets linked partners overlap or start before 0 (8929d1164, #8416).
  • Studio: A refused delete no longer brings back another delete still saving (c1f4a193a, #8413).
  • Engine: A capture proxy request whose name will not resolve is reported as failed (75c83d877, #8409).
  • Studio: A sealed undo of a GSAP-animated layer's style shows in place (c3a673990, #8320).
  • Media Use: A project manifest's provider claim never writes a sound credit (ec1839e4a, #8410).
  • Studio Server: A file the drive never answers no longer holds a project's first history copy (267ffc46c, #8187).
  • Studio: Fx badges in a sealed preview show the clip's look, crop and duck (8a69ea039, #8411).
  • Studio: A paused untagged video shows the colours the export renders (71907274a, #8405).
  • CLI: Show copy file names in check progress lines as visible text (4a5a5eb27, #8407).
  • Studio: A delete and its ripple shift save as one edit (7ec38388b, #8404).
  • Studio: The poster rendered for the next open no longer delays a clip's picture (da46c89e2, #8400).
  • Studio Server: A file the drive never answers no longer holds a project's first copy (71dc6f2fe, #8403).
  • CLI: The compile cache lives only in the private HyperFrames cache folder (8127bb885, #8401).

Internal

  • Engine: The frame-tag checks get the time a real encode needs (2b4706f19, #8415).
  • Studio Server: The Windows workflow fails when the link tests cannot run (b008e37b4, #8395).

v0.9.79 Stable2026-11-21T22:37:11Z

HyperFrames v0.9.79

Released on 2026-11-21.

Trimming a main-track clip with Ripple on now closes the gap. Media files that sit outside a project are no longer reused, credited or written through a linked folder.

Fixes

  • Media Use: A transcript cut named inside the project is never written through a linked folder (4e873f5a1, #8398).
  • Studio: Trimming a main-track clip with Ripple on closes the gap (8222489f0, #8394).
  • Media Use: A manifest record whose path leaves the project is not reused or credited (e3a0797a3, #8396).
  • Studio Server: A preview asset whose read cannot be recorded answers not found (22b394c66, #8385).
  • Ci: Release checks refuse a patch release that carries a breaking change (e87babf26, #8392).

v0.9.78 Stable2026-11-21T20:28:21Z

HyperFrames v0.9.78

Released on 2026-11-21.

Undo after an agent step now brings back the files it changed. A catalog gap report on Windows never puts a query your shell would run into a command, and renders sharing one capture proxy keep their connections.

Features

  • Engine: A late image's load time is logged when it arrives (e65b11e5c, #8372).

Fixes

  • CLI: Show control characters as text in notices that repeat a failure reason (c75860ddf, #8387).
  • Core: A Windows rename onto a folder that exists fails at once instead of waiting 1.3 s (7cb548e45, #8389).
  • Core: Show text direction marks and typed text as visible text in printed lines (f01d1f32b, #8384).
  • Studio Server: History keeps undo steps in order when file times and its clock disagree (63e700de1, #8383).
  • Core: A long text's name is cut at a word, not inside one (19587337f, #8388).
  • CLI: Check reports its own deadlines as check_deadline (d78e9a5f3, #8378).
  • CLI: Windows kills end only the process the CLI started, never a reused pid (93f2fcca4, #8382).
  • Player: Stop the preview reloading itself after a quick seek, and stalling at cuts on a slower connection (120689e3c, #8339).
  • CLI: Print check and validate setup commands on their own lines (88f7d4052, #8381).
  • Studio Server: The preview file check resolves links on volumes that refuse native realpath (40cc86252, #8377).
  • CLI: Print capture setup commands on their own lines (281f1a2a4, #8374).
  • Studio Server: An agent step waits for history's first copies, so undo brings old files back (fbbf5ee67, #8375).
  • Studio Server: History refusals carry a closed reason, so callers tell them from failures (d5881e8aa, #8376).
  • Studio Server: A timed-out history save says it may still land, so callers don't retry blindly (6b74cfa9f, #8300).
  • CLI: Read --json before the command name the same as after it (9a618b798, #8365).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in capture, figma, usage and notice lines (7fcc4909b, #8366).
  • Studio: Adding or removing a text field keeps the spaces between words (e8f52dc86, #8369).
  • CLI: A received-files write waits out a held lock instead of failing on Windows (f74e1545c, #8367).
  • CLI: A stop reads the process tree from /proc on Linux, so load cannot hide a child (a2df08e49, #8363).
  • Engine: A capture proxy shared by many browsers no longer drops tunnels at 128 (5e16c18b1, #8362).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in init, skills and render printed lines (8bc73a0bd, #8352).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in more commands' printed lines (7a1163fc9, #8353).
  • Studio Server: A preview file request no longer waits on a silent drive on the event loop (7cb605c78, #8254).

Catalog

  • CLI: A catalog gap report hands back a line only when every shell reads the query as text (414557262, #8373).

Internal

  • CLI: The probe-cache snapshot test asks 16 times of an 8 s clip, not 40 of 20 s (c7a6c9315, #8390).
  • Lint: Linear scans are held to characters read and one coarse time bound (e0d844030, #8386).
  • Studio: The marquee-past-the-cap check waits as long as the frame takes to answer (b0d9f3615, #7757).
  • Studio Server: A held-file edit is checked for blocking waits, not wall-clock gaps (29a5003ee, #8236).
  • Studio Server: A held save proves it never sleeps the thread, not a 100 ms stall bound (ba81f6793, #8359).

Other Changes

  • CLI: The proxy fix and its Node.js 22.19 floor came out again before this release; they ship in v0.10.0 (296031974, #8311, 66156bf35, #8391).

kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill ↗ 12 releases · 12 stable
v4.7.0 Stable2026-11-22T14:28:21Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.6.5.

Added

  • audio.py --track FILE: several audio tracks in one file, each with a name and a language (#420). The input's own sound (--audio-stream) and the first audio stream of each --track become one audio stream apiece after the picture (stream-copied; an audio input writes none), in .mov, .mkv or .mp4. Each is written as it is: its own channel layout, rate and length (nothing mixed, resampled, padded or trimmed), as 24-bit PCM in .mov and .mkv and AAC in .mp4. --track-name NAME (also --track-title) and --track-lang CODE (also --track-language) are repeated in track order, the input's sound first; a language is an ISO 639-2 code of three lowercase letters. The result's tracks lists {index, file, title, language, channels, duration} read back from the file, a track_tags step checks the names and languages against it (.mov keeps only the languages QuickTime has a code for, and says so by failing the step), and a track longer than the picture is kept whole with a track_longer_than_picture warning. Refused, with error.reason: an output that is not .mov, .mkv or .mp4 (track_container; a WAV holds one stream, and FFmpeg's MXF muxer stores no names or languages and cuts every track to the shortest), a flag that changes the sound (track_rebuilds_sound), more titles or languages than tracks (track_labels) and a language that is not three letters (track_language). A BWF or multitrack WAV with a start timecode is not written.
  • feat: audio.py --track adds named, language-tagged audio tracks (#420) (#714)

What's Changed

  • feat: audio.py --track adds named, language-tagged audio tracks (#420) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/714

Full Changelog: https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/compare/v4.6.5...v4.7.0

v4.6.5 Stable2026-11-22T12:05:05Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.6.4.

Fixed

  • The mixing tools get #711's relabel too. grid.py, join.py, multicam.py, broll.py and overlay.py build each source's BT.709 conversion through their own helper, which 4.6.4's fix did not reach. On FFmpeg 9 an HDR source in them could still be written with a bt709 stream under bt2020/PQ container tags. One helper is now the BT.709 line's filter for every tool.

Added

  • A guard for the colour class (test_guards.ColorGuard, three ledger entries). Every tool that writes video runs on an HDR RGB source, and any output whose tags mix SDR and HDR fails it.
  • fix: the mixing tools' BT.709 chains get the FFmpeg 9 relabel, and the colour class has a guard (#711) (#713)

What's Changed

  • fix: the mixing tools' BT.709 chains get the FFmpeg 9 relabel, and the colour class has a guard (#711) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/713

v4.6.4 Stable2026-11-22T10:50:02Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.6.3.

Fixed

  • On FFmpeg 9 a BT.709 preset over an HDR source wrote conflicting tags (#711). The encoder's VUI said bt709 and the mp4/mov colr box said bt2020/smpte2084, because FFmpeg 9 takes the container's colour from the frames. The line now relabels the frames' primaries and transfer (setparams, no pixel changes), so both say bt709. FFmpeg 6.1 to 8 wrote bt709 already and are unchanged.
  • fix: a BT.709 line's container tags agree with its stream on FFmpeg 9 (#711) (#712)

What's Changed

  • fix: a BT.709 line's container tags agree with its stream on FFmpeg 9 (#711) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/712

v4.6.3 Stable2026-11-22T09:40:00Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.6.2.

Fixed

  • An HDR or BT.2020 RGB source is converted with the matrix its line is tagged with, and a 10-bit master keeps its HDR tags (#559). export.py --preset youtube (and the other 8-bit presets, and grid.py) tag BT.709 and made such a source's YUV with FFmpeg's BT.601 default: the red C03028 read CE3E26 by its bt709 tag; it is now converted with the BT.709 matrix and reads C02E27 (the primaries and transfer are still not converted, and the colour_not_converted warning still says so). --preset prores, dnxhr-*, prores4444, prores4444xq, vp9-alpha and graphics.py / overlay.py --keep-alpha wrote no colour tag for it (ffprobe: unknown/unknown/unknown; the 8-bit DNxHR lines said bt709 over BT.601 pixels): they now carry the source's primaries and transfer and bt2020nc, the matrix the picture was converted with, as a YUV source does.
  • fix: an HDR RGB source is converted with the matrix its line is tagged with, and a 10-bit line keeps its HDR tags (#559) (#708)

What's Changed

  • fix: an HDR RGB source is converted with the matrix its line is tagged with, and a 10-bit line keeps its HDR tags (#559) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/708

v4.6.2 Stable2026-11-22T08:05:14Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.6.1.

Changed

  • Tools start faster (#679). The FCPXML writer imports xml.sax.saxutils, which pulls in urllib.request, http.client and ssl, only when it writes, instead of every tool importing it at start-up. No tool's output changes. cut.py --help, median of 31 interleaved runs: 119 ms -> 83 ms. Importing the transcription and text-layout modules on first use saved 3 to 4 ms more and made mock.patch of a re-exported name leave the patch in place on exit, so it was left out. No probe cache: a run probes each input once.
  • fix: audio.py --duck lifted at a random point and differed per run (#671) (#705)
  • test: a killed command reports what its processes were doing (#533) (#704)

v4.6.0 Stable2026-11-22T06:02:53Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.5.3.

Added

  • join.py --audio-lead S (J-cut) and --audio-lag S (L-cut) (#675). With --transition none, the next clip's sound starts S seconds before its picture (lead), or the previous clip's sound runs S seconds past the picture change (lag), at every boundary. Sync is kept, so each boundary costs S seconds of a clip: two clips of 8 s and 6 s with S = 0.5 join to 13.5 s. S is whole frames of the output rate; the result carries audio_lead_seconds / audio_lag_seconds and expected_duration is the shortened length. Refused, with error.reason: with a transition, with --copy, both flags together, and S under a frame or not shorter than a clip. SKILL.md routes "J-cut", "L-cut", "cutaway", "cut-in", "reaction cut", "insert" and "smash cut" to their tools; references/requests.md has the full table of editors' names.
  • feat: J-cut and L-cut in join.py (#675) (#701)

What's Changed

  • feat: J-cut and L-cut in join.py (#675) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/701

v4.5.3 Stable2026-11-22T03:20:15Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.5.2.

Fixed

  • cut.py --segments, cut.py --remove and silence.py move the chapters with the ranges they keep (#696). A join of several ranges kept the source's chapters at the source's times, past the output's end. Found by the new time guard, not by a report.
  • Any tool that changes the length and leaves chapters behind now says so (#696). The common result writer warns chapters_not_retimed when an output of another length carries chapters and its tool declared no time map.

Added

  • Every class of defect fixed three times has a guard that runs every tool (#698). tests/test_guards.py runs all the tools (the multi-input ones included) on a 16:9, 5.1, chaptered, subtitled clip with stdin left open, and fails on: chapters left at the source's times (time), a lost channel or a picture mostly bars or cropped away with nothing said (claim), a dry run that reports a measurement, or a record that judges a file it does not name (not_measured), the sound before the picture or a result whose first line is not wrote (surface), a tool that waits on an open stdin (automation). Each was checked by putting the old defect back. docs/defect-guards.json lists them, and revert_proof.py refuses a fix of a class with three ledger entries and no guard, so the next class that keeps coming back gets one before its fourth fix.
  • fix: every class of defect fixed three times has a guard that runs every tool (#700)

What's Changed

  • fix: every class of defect fixed three times has a guard that runs every tool by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/700

v4.5.2 Stable2026-11-21T23:53:15Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.5.1.

Fixed

  • A crop that keeps less than half of the picture is said (#688). fit.py, export.py and join.py with --fit crop into a frame of the other shape (a 16:9 clip into 9:16 keeps 32%) now warn picture_mostly_cropped with the fraction kept, as a pad that leaves the picture small has since 4.3.4. The file is unchanged.
  • Chapters follow a change of time (#696). pad.py --start, reverse.py, speedramp.py, freeze.py and fit.py --method speed|trim left a source's chapters at the source's times; they are now moved, mirrored or scaled with the picture, clipped to the output, and a chapter left with no length is dropped. MP4 and MOV still start their first chapter at 0 (the container's chapter track has no gap); Matroska keeps the exact times.
  • fix: a crop that keeps less than half of the picture is said, and chapters follow a change of time (#697)

What's Changed

  • fix: a crop that keeps less than half of the picture is said, and chapters follow a change of time by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/697

v4.5.1 Stable2026-11-21T22:59:24Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.5.0.

Fixed

  • A forced frame rate relabels the start timecode in every tool that takes --fps (#470). proxy.py, denoise.py, crop.py, redact.py, straighten.py and sphere.py --fps 50 on a 25 fps source starting 01:00:00:12 kept 01:00:00:12; it is 01:00:00:24 at 50 now, as fit.py already wrote.
  • audio.py keeps the picture as the first stream (#470). With the picture kept (--voice, --music, --replace, ...) the sound was stream 0.
  • broll.py --audio b|mix keeps a 5.1 A-roll in 5.1 (#470). It was folded to stereo with verified: true. Mono and stereo A-rolls are mixed in stereo as before.
  • fix: a forced frame rate relabels the timecode, audio.py keeps the picture first, broll keeps 5.1 (#695)

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.39CC Switch v4.0.39 Stable2026-11-22T13:42:30Z

CC Switch v4.0.39

本版本以修复为主。API Key 输入框加了复制按钮；备份可以设置总大小上限，超过时从最旧的开始删；Claude Code 经 Codex OAuth 使用 GPT-6.2 Sol / Astra 时，系统提示的缓存命中率大幅提高。

另外修复了 Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop 的原生供应商卡住不出字时，要等最长 600 秒才故障转移；很长的流式响应让用量和日志收集占用的内存一直增长；一键导入链接合并配置失败后仍弹出确认框；只装了 Codex 桌面版时，插件页显示「未安装 Codex」；插件页给没装的应用建了空配置文件夹；OpenCode Go 的 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 缺少 max 思考档位；快速点击时弹出层和下拉菜单闪烁等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

API Key 输入框可以一键复制

供应商表单的 API Key 输入框旁边多了复制按钮（#9232），复制的是去掉首尾空格后的 Key，成功或失败都会提示。输入框为空或不可编辑时不显示。

备份总大小上限

备份设置里新增「备份总大小上限」（#9011，相关 #6706），默认「不限制」，升级后不会删任何备份。

  • 设置上限后，备份文件夹超过这个大小时，会从最旧的备份开始删除。
  • 最新的一份备份和恢复正在使用的备份始终保留，所以单份备份比上限还大时，文件夹仍可能超过上限。
  • 上限在下次备份或每日维护时生效。每日维护还会清理备份中途崩溃留下、超过 24 小时的临时文件。

变更

  • Claude Code 经 Codex OAuth 使用 GPT-6.2 Sol / Astra 时缓存命中率更高（#9848，见 #9847）：系统提示改放在第一条 developer 消息里，ChatGPT 后端会把它和对话一起缓存。实测从第二轮起命中率约 98%。其他模型和用 API Key 的路由不变。
  • 隐私过滤器更快（#9826）：过滤结果不变。

修复

  • Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop 的原生供应商卡住不出字时，最长要等 600 秒才故障转移（#9754）：这类流式请求用的是非流式请求的超时，现在和其他流式请求一样用首字超时（默认 60 秒）。
  • 很长或一直不结束的流式响应，让用量和日志收集占用的内存一直增长（#9846，见 #9843）：缓冲现在最多 64 MiB，遇到超大的事件后也能继续解析后面的内容。
  • 一键导入链接合并配置失败后，仍弹出确认框（#9844，见 #9841）：现在只提示错误，不再弹确认框。
  • 只装了 Codex 桌面版时，插件页显示「未安装 Codex」（见 #9850）：比如 Windows 上只装了 Codex 桌面版（MSIX）。现在找不到单独安装的 codex 时，插件页等用到 Codex 命令行的地方会改用桌面版自带的那份。「应用」页的安装、升级和卸载仍只管单独安装的 Codex CLI。
  • 插件页给没装的应用建了空配置文件夹（见 #9852）：打开插件页时，没装 Codex、Grok Build、OpenClaw、Pi 或 MiniMax Code 也会建出 ~/.codex、~/.grok 等空文件夹。现在找到命令行工具后才建，没装的照常显示「未安装」。
  • OpenCode Go 的 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 缺少 max 思考档位（见 #9853）：预设现在按 OpenCode Go 的模型列表写明 low、high、max 三档，OpenCode 不再退回通用的 low / medium / high。
  • 快速点击时，弹出层和下拉菜单闪烁（#9176，见 #9175）：在打开或关闭的动画过程中再点一下，不会再连着切换两次。

致谢

感谢 @H15teve（GPT-6.2 缓存命中）、@minimote（复制 API Key）、@zayokami（备份总大小上限）、@vastStage（流式请求超时）、@HaiYangBG1（流式响应内存、一键导入失败提示、测试隔离）、@YUZHEthefool（隐私过滤器提速）、@Delta1035（弹出层闪烁）、@allenxu09（评审修改）、@SaladDay（代码整理）的贡献。

感谢 @IndexEeve、@Thanatos-Z、@beiqixingnang、@muchenkezhan 报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

View release →

cua-driver-rs-v0.58.1cua-driver-rs: v0.58.1 Prerelease2026-11-22T11:05:14Z

Cua Driver 0.58.1

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • keep the macOS agent cursor under a window covering its background target. (#8511) reported by @r33drichards.

Contributors

Thanks to @r33drichards for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.58.1-nightly.20261122.44984672738 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Cua Driver 0.57.0

Summary

This release adds 1 features and includes 3 fixes.

Features

  • sample the Hyprland cursor during recording. (#8084) Thanks @austindixson.

Fixes

  • export EmbeddedDriverDiagnostics from the embedded subpath. (#8068)
  • print get_guide text from the call command. (#8106) Thanks @Alx8g.
  • label macOS controls through AXTitleUIElement. (#8114) reported by @SWSAmor.

Contributors

Thanks to @Alx8g, @austindixson, @SWSAmor for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.56.8

Summary

This release includes 3 fixes.

Fixes

  • re-pin the macOS agent cursor overlay when the active app changes. (#8478)
  • press a real Enter for a trailing newline in browser_type. (#7732) Thanks @sahiljagtap08; reported by @Ed-Key.
  • report the PID of the daemon serving the selected socket. (#7626)

Contributors

Thanks to @Ed-Key, @sahiljagtap08 for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.56.6

Summary

This release includes 5 fixes.

Fixes

  • build every Windows crate against windows 0.61. (#8444)
  • match list_windows app filter against unlocalized bundle names. (#8358) reported by @ymqy.
  • build move_cursor action records in its producers. (#8080)
  • report the window covering a verified bring_to_front target. (#8093) reported by @ayushsuletiya.
  • send a background pixel click past an element without AXPress. (#8159) reported by @Xiaojiuxingxiaodu.

Contributors

Thanks to @ayushsuletiya, @Xiaojiuxingxiaodu, @ymqy for contributing to this release.

codewhale-hq/Codewhale ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.10.3Codewhale v0.10.3 Stable2026-11-22T12:11:33Z

What's in v0.10.3

Codewhale v0.10.3 brings a unified Plugins surface with Creator mode ([features] plugin_creator) and unmodified DeepSeek Harness (@deepseek-ai/dsh-*) plugin compatibility, an upgraded Auto-Review guardian with workspace boundaries, soft rules, sub-agent return review, and one-use retry approvals, and a single Shift+Tab permission dial defaulting to Accept Edits (Ask → Accept Edits → Plan → Auto-Review → Full Access). It moves the ACP server and external-agent sessions (including Qwen Code and GitHub Copilot CLI) onto the official Agent Client Protocol SDK, publishes native signed HarmonyOS ARM64 release assets, adds a verified signed model-and-provider registry channel alongside unified model-ID resolution and managed /v1/models pricing, updates design tokens to 5.4.0 with a docked single-line approval strip, folded turn activity, live working-row light sweeps and 13 hex-lattice dot-whale forms, adds claude-org billing auth, show_data transcript charts, codewhale sessions migrate, DeepSeek Chatbot web search and speech tools, receipted self-updates and codewhale uninstall, and lands reliability, CJK/Unicode, Windows, and sandbox security fixes.

Install

Recommended — official GitHub release

New macOS/Linux install (checksummed binaries from this release):

curl -fsSL https://codewhale.net/install.sh | CODEWHALE_VERSION="v0.10.3" sh
"$HOME/.local/bin/codewhale" --version

For Windows, use the matching installer or archive below. For an existing direct install, run codewhale update; it prints the executable path and keeps newer builds. If the install directory is occupied by a different build, use the fresh-directory migration in the installation guide.

Secondary packaging — npm and Cargo

npm install -g codewhale
# or build from source
cargo install codewhale-cli --locked

The wrapper downloads the matched codewhale and codew command assets from this Release. Both contain the same compiled runtime.

Docker / GHCR

docker run --rm -it \
  -e DEEPSEEK_API_KEY="$DEEPSEEK_API_KEY" \
  -v codewhale-home:/home/codewhale/.codewhale \
  ghcr.io/codewhale-hq/codewhale:v0.10.3

The image exposes the same runtime as both codewhale and codew. The latest tag is also updated on release.

Cargo (Linux / macOS)

cargo install codewhale-cli --locked

The Cargo package installs codewhale. Cargo cannot create a second command alias from one binary target; users who want the shorter spelling can add a codew symlink to that installed executable. The npm, Homebrew, archive, shell-installer, and container channels install both command names directly.

Manual download — platform archives (recommended)

Each archive below contains the same runtime under the codewhale and codew command names, plus an install script:

Platform Archive Install script
Linux x64 codewhale-linux-x64.tar.gz install.sh
Linux ARM64 codewhale-linux-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
Android ARM64 (Termux) codewhale-android-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
HarmonyOS ARM64 (native) codewhale-ohos-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
macOS x64 codewhale-macos-x64.tar.gz install.sh
macOS ARM codewhale-macos-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
Windows x64 (installer) CodeWhaleSetup.exe NSIS setup
Windows x64 codewhale-windows-x64.zip install.bat
Windows x64 (portable) codewhale-windows-x64-portable.zip —
Windows ARM64 codewhale-windows-arm64.zip install.bat
Windows ARM64 (portable) codewhale-windows-arm64-portable.zip —

Unix (Linux / macOS):

tar xzf codewhale-<platform>.tar.gz
cd codewhale-<platform>
./install.sh

Windows:

  • For the installer path, run CodeWhaleSetup.exe; it installs codewhale.exe, codew.exe, and codewhale.bat under %LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\CodeWhale\bin, adds that directory to the current-user PATH, and creates a Start Menu shortcut that prefers Windows Terminal (wt.exe) when it is installed.
  • Extract the archive for your machine: codewhale-windows-x64.zip or codewhale-windows-arm64.zip
  • Double-click codewhale.bat (not the raw .exe) to launch
  • Run install.bat to copy the binaries and launcher to %USERPROFILE%\bin
  • Add %USERPROFILE%\bin to your PATH

The portable Windows archive skips the install script — extract and run codewhale.bat from any directory. The NSIS installer is currently unsigned and may trigger Windows SmartScreen until a signing certificate is wired into the release pipeline.

Each platform also has bare, unarchived codewhale-<platform> and codew-<platform> assets. The seven codewhale-tui-<platform> filenames attached to v0.9.5 are byte-identical compatibility copies used only to let already-installed v0.9.4 clients discover and cross this single-binary transition; current installers do not expose a third runtime. The legacy npm package deepseek-tui is deprecated and is not republished. For migration from v0.8.x legacy binary names, see docs/REBRAND.md.

Verify (recommended)

Download the checksum manifests from this Release and verify:

# Linux — archive bundles
sha256sum -c codewhale-bundles-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

# Linux — individual binaries
sha256sum -c codewhale-artifacts-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

# macOS
shasum -a 256 -c codewhale-bundles-sha256.txt --ignore-missing
shasum -a 256 -c codewhale-artifacts-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

unixwin/niubash ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.5.1Niubash v1.5.1 Stable2026-11-22T11:41:56Z

Fixed

  • Deep-audit host fix wave (1.5.1-bound, 2026-11-04): seven non-interactive host findings fixed (#886) — including corrupt plugin-spec errors now naming the file and the cause; $BASH_ARGV0 seed no longer clobbered by host startup and NIU_COMP_WORDS no longer duplicated (#887, with the engine-side compspec fixes in rubash #2756); frozen interactive config specs (NIU_BINDKEYS/NIU_COMPDEFS) re-applied on setup re-run (#891, closing #889/#890). The remaining host finding (#888, exported HISTFILE) is a recorded owner decision. Engine-side counterparts landed in rubash's deep-audit wave (see the rubash changelog).
  • Windows interactive login shells read ~/.niubashrc again (niubash#1057): --login/-l (and the argv[0] - seed) no longer divert the interactive startup face onto the GNU profile chain — niu --login -i now sources the generated plugin loader and ~/.niubashrc exactly like niu -i (--rcfile and NIU_RC_POLICY apply unchanged), restoring the standing contract "Windows accepts the flags with unchanged behavior (no login concept)". GNU shell.c:1833's "the login profile chain replaces the interactive-RC alternative" is a deliberate product fork on this face, not a GNU-alignment gap: on a bare Windows home none of the chain's files exists, so --login -i used to source nothing at all. Deliberate side effects: ① a staged GNU profile file (.bash_profile/.bash_login/.profile) no longer runs on the Windows interactive face; ② the #590 expectation flips on Windows — an interactive login shell loads loader + rc and no chain; ③ the non-interactive -c face is unchanged — -l -c still sources the GNU chain (niubash#399/#587/#602) — and POSIX mode keeps the chain on every platform.
  • Engine and bundled-commands line rises to 1.5.1: the rubash pin takes rubash master 649b3be3 (v1.5.1 — the 703-issue GNU Bash 5.3.20 differential wave, see the rubash changelog), the ruline pin takes ruline main 4d0aefa8, and the bundled WinuxCmd line is 1.2.2. Also in this wave: bind -x conhost chord dispatch and READLINE_POINT restore (#1065, closing #1063) and the env.exe distribution fix through the winuxcmd bundle (#1062).

  • 新增实验性时间线插件，可查看智能体任务树、阻塞事项和进展报告，点击进度条可展开任务树，任务与报告语言可选跟随对话。 @Magolor
  • 新增代码审阅页面，可集中查看本人相关的 GitHub PR，浏览描述和变更，并在页内与智能体协作审阅。 @LegGasai
  • 代码审阅插件新增扩展机制，插件可添加自己的审阅视图和行注解，并在工具栏、文件菜单和评论框中加入自定义操作；同时支持评审和合并 GitLab。 @LegGasai
  • 新增会话状态区域，可集中查看工作目录、变更请求、子智能体、任务和插件状态，支持在较窄窗口停靠及拖动调整分区顺序。 @LegGasai
  • 新增会话已读/未读状态，完成后提醒未读，可用菜单或快捷键切换；改善最近会话切换并保留侧栏状态。 @Saafo, @imccyu
  • Web 连接模式新增远程登录，可使用独立登录链接或设备配对方式访问，并提供标准化连接鉴权 API 供插件注册 @EricMoin, @imccyu
  • 插件页支持统一管理连接器账号，MCP 支持 OAuth 授权与凭据自动刷新；CLI 应用插件共用账号管理和上游技能下载能力。 @grllll
  • 新增可选的 Linear、Notion、腾讯文档、飞书、企业微信、腾讯会议和钉钉连接器插件，可在插件页连接或授权后在对话中使用相应服务。 @grllll

体验优化

  • 简化模型菜单中的智能体团队入口，开关与开销说明移到二级面板。 @Yifffan
  • 修复子智能体模型选择与消息来源识别，授权设置及时生效，并完善结果汇报和关键发现通知。 @Dudu-0223
  • 改善子代理、分支会话阅读与创建体验，继承历史与新消息之间显示分隔线 @turtle2099, @Yifffan, @imccyu
  • 加快会话列表与 @ 引用候选的加载。 @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • 优化搜索列表的模糊与跨语言匹配，例如输入 cpt 可找到 /compact；新建会话时可按名称或目录路径搜索工作区。 @turtle1999, @acehinnnqru
  • 优化自动化任务的推荐、删除确认和会话标记，新增「每周回顾」推荐。 @ZiyaZhang, @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • 优化 DeepSeek 图片上传，多张图片并发上传，遇到限流时自动重试。 @CreatixChu
  • 改善 web_fetch 网络失败时的提示，显示可读的失败原因和处理建议。 @turtle2099, @Elevator14B
  • 优化代码审阅与工作树体验，变更请求列表更紧凑、可折叠，GitHub PR 状态更新更及时，变更浏览更清晰；工作树可自定义分支名、从远端分支创建，并在首条消息发出后创建。 @LegGasai, @imccyu, @turtle2099
  • 改善长会话中流式输出、轮次导航和拖动右侧栏时的响应，减少大型 Markdown 表格和列表在对话与文档预览中的卡顿。 @turtle2099, @imccyu, @std-microblock
  • 优化界面显示与列表交互，统一图标、对齐和间距，改善侧栏滚动与拖动排序，修复多处显示问题。 @turtle2099, @grllll, @yixiangihsiang, @shigma, @std4453, @oraluben, @winewill, @07akioni
  • Office 技能统一通过 /office 提供 Word、PowerPoint 和 Excel 指引，旧格式命令和自定义配置需迁移；内置 Python 提供 pypdf，Office 指引也说明如何用它处理已有 PDF。 @yudshj
  • 可从对话中的压缩记录在右侧栏预览手动或自动生成的上下文压缩摘要。 @yudshj
  • 桌面版可从应用菜单直接打开「设置」，菜单同步显示当前生效的单步快捷键。 @lsdsjy
  • 在空白输入框按 ↑ 可召回本会话已发送的消息文本，再用 ↑/↓ 浏览，不覆盖正在编辑的草稿。 @std4453
  • 文件右键菜单新增「添加到对话」，可直接插入引用并继续编辑，不会自动发送。 @Kaige-Gao
  • 可将普通会话从列表拖到右侧栏打开并继续对话，无需离开当前会话。 @turtle2099, @Yifffan, @liukx0205
  • 可在「模型」设置中自定义内置目录模型的推理档位，关闭自定义后沿用目录默认档位。 @LegGasai
  • 优化共享会话在各功能中的支持度，读者可以参与更多操作。 @mektpoy

问题修复

  • 修复切换模型后首个请求超出上下文时会话卡住的问题，自动恢复和 /compact 改用最近回答的模型生成摘要。 @LegGasai
  • 修复 DeepSeek 图片上传问题：服务无响应时不再每次请求都长时间等待，取消上传不再导致运行时退出。 @CreatixChu, @LegGasai
  • 修复在 Python wheel 等单文件运行时中恢复旧版会话失败的问题。 @Magolor
  • 修复在 PTC 程序中通过 gh 或 git push 操作的 PR 不出现在代码审阅面板的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复 macOS 上输入框按 Home/End 会滚动对话的问题，现在移到当前行首或行末。 @ZiyaZhang
  • 修复浏览器拒绝剪贴板写入时复制按钮无效的问题。 @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • 修复 Windows 安装完成时拖动导致输入卡住和关闭按钮失效，以及卸载后残留系统登记、清理失败未报告的问题。 @winewill
  • 修复 Windows 桌面版用「在应用中打开」启动 VS Code、Cursor 等编辑器失败的问题。 @turtle2099, @huoyaoyuan, @turtle1999
  • 修复桌面版打开用量、充值页面后 Cmd+W 无法关闭窗口的问题。 @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • 修复只创建或编辑过自动化任务的会话也显示时钟标记的问题。 @Magolor
  • 修复切换会话时草稿附件丢失的问题，上传和图片预览也会保留。 @mektpoy
  • 修正插件管理的重启指引，变更已生效时不再要求重启。 @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • 修复不存在的 @ 路径被显示为文件引用的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复实验性个性化页面无法在后台创建的问题。 @Dudu-0223
  • 修复 read 和 glob 在处理大文件或大量结果后持续占用过多内存的问题。 @turtle2099, @imccyu
  • 修复新建文件引用和文件标签在会话切换工作目录后可能指向错误文件的问题；依赖 fileAddressFor 返回相对地址的插件需适配。 @LegGasai
  • 修复智能体团队任务领取失败时提示不明确的问题，已完成或删除的任务不再提示写入范围重叠。 @Dudu-0223
  • 修复 Web 应用通用安装图标显示为直角方块的问题，改用透明圆角。 @EricMoin
  • 修复桌面诊断历史因文件身份误判或 Windows 临时打开失败而遗漏部分操作记录的问题。 @Chinesezjc
  • 修复手动输入 @ 路径时字符顺序打乱、光标位置错误的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复经 pi 接入的模型发送超限图片时未正确缩图的问题，发送前按尺寸和字节上限缩图，原始附件不变；自定义图片配置需按升级指南适配。 @CreatixChu
  • 修复在同一主机的多个端口登录 Web 版后，旧登录状态累积可能导致页面打不开的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复点击模型选择菜单里的分组标题或空白处时，菜单意外关闭的问题。 @shigma

其他变更

  • 调整系统提示词与 SDK 时区处理：系统指令按来源增量更新，压缩后重建；SDK 普通提示可逐条指定时区，未指定时使用可识别的运行时时区，自定义集成需按升级指南适配。 @Tuyixiang, @yudshj
  • 同步 Cordis 上游至 4.0.0-rc.12，并更新 Loader 与 Include；调整相关 API、YAML 解析和配置校验，插件与自定义配置需按升级指南迁移。 @shigma
  • 调整 Cordis 检查工具，将创造模式的多个工具合并为 cordis_inspect，并在标准模式默认启用，支持查询运行中的 DSH 版本。 @turtle1999, @turtle2099, @Dudu-0223
  • 原 Cordis 插件配置由仅支持后端读取调整为同时支持前端读取，并对 volatile 类型配置支持实时同步 @imccyu
  • 启用 HMR 时，--patch 覆盖文件修改后无需重启即可生效。 @shigma
  • 调整插件设置与启停入口，插件页可直接打开设置，「内置插件」设置跳转到插件管理。 @turtle2099, @LegGasai, @turtle1999
  • 调整 Computer Use，macOS 统一系统键鼠输入授权，Windows 新增实验性支持；并改善操作稳定性与设置指引。 @huoyaoyuan, @agicy, @CreatixChu, @Dudu-0223, @kermanx
  • 调整 Computer Use 的接入方式，可通过 cua-mcp 以 MCP 服务器形式提供外部调用，标准模式以及 SDK、headless 等接入也默认可用 cua_run_code。 @LegGasai, @turtle1999
  • 调整 PTC 运行与结果展示：Node PTC 默认在调用间保留变量，支持重复声明、用 inspect 展示文本、图片和工具结果，并截断超限输出；移除 Python PTC 支持。 @kermanx, @imccyu, @turtle1999, @turtle2099, @Dudu-0223, @sjw1231, @tianyicui
  • 调整各类工具的失败结果，统一携带结构化错误名与错误码，部分已有错误码按升级指南改名。 @sjw1231
  • 规范模块的导出入口，移除仅供测试的导出和旧子路径，类型改从 /types 或包根导入。 @imccyu
  • 调整会话状态迁到新包 dsh-client-ui-session-status，读取会话状态的插件需在 inject 中声明。 @imccyu
  • 调整客户端插件产物结构，样式改由框架在挂载时安装，CSS Modules 类名格式变化。 @imccyu
  • 调整会话 cwd，文件视图与 workspace-write 边界随之切换，工作树 Git 写操作需额外授权；移除 working_directory 和委派 cwd 参数，工作树改用 enter_worktree / exit_worktree，旧会话恢复到原项目。 @LegGasai
  • 默认开启个性化推荐插件，推荐直接显示在智能体回答中 @ZiyaZhang, @Dudu-0223
  • 恢复实验性「持久终端」的启停与详情入口，安装后默认关闭。 @LegGasai
  • 调整会话存储，支持实验性 SQLite 后端，需手动配置启用，默认仍使用 JSONL。 @liukx0205
  • DeepSeek 的 API 密钥提供方现显示为「DeepSeek API」，以区别于 DeepSeek 账号；模型、搜索和网页抓取统一通过 ctx.deepseekAuth 获取授权，自定义组合与相关插件需按升级指南迁移。 @turtle2099, @LegGasai, @liyao, @yudshj
  • 调整分支会话创建接口，自定义分支调用需同时提供完整历史和父会话来源（parentSource）。 @liukx0205
  • 扩展会话工具，提供可选的只读工作区和会话列表；Web 的跨会话消息工具默认关闭，启用后智能体可按用户要求向同一运行时的其他普通会话发送文本，支持跨工作区。 @Dudu-0223
  • 调整 SDK 恢复会话接口，支持指定 permissionMode 覆盖原有权限。 @koalazf99
  • 调整 list_agents，同时返回子智能体并发占用、上限和成员列表；读取结构化结果的调用方需改从 agents 字段查找成员。 @Dudu-0223
  • 扩展输入框接口，插件可为不同会话的空白输入框提供行内提示，不改动用户草稿。 @EricMoin
  • 新增可选的 MCP 工具按需加载模式，减少模型请求的上下文占用。 @grllll

<h3 id="en-v0.2.3-alpha.1">New Features</h3>

  • The plugin market can add Git repositories, including private ones, as plugin sources. by @acehinnnqru
  • Add an experimental Timeline plugin for agent task trees, blockers, and progress reports, with a clickable progress strip and an option for task and report language to follow the chat. by @Magolor
  • Add a code review page for GitHub pull requests involving you, with descriptions, change views, and in-page collaboration with an agent. by @LegGasai
  • Plugins can now extend code review with custom views, line annotations, and actions in the toolbar, file menu, and comment box. You can also review and merge GitLab merge requests. by @LegGasai
  • Add a session status area for the working directory, change requests, subagents, tasks, and plugin status. It can dock in narrower windows and supports drag-to-reorder sections. by @LegGasai
  • Add read/unread status for sessions, mark finished sessions unread, and switch the status from the session menu or a keyboard shortcut; improve returning to recent sessions while preserving sidebar state. by @Saafo, @imccyu
  • Sign in remotely in Web connection mode with a dedicated sign-in link or device pairing. Plugins can register authentication providers through a standard connection API. by @EricMoin, @imccyu
  • Manage connector accounts from the Plugins page with MCP OAuth authorization and automatic credential refresh; CLI application plugins share account management and upstream skill downloads. by @grllll
  • Add optional connector plugins for Linear, Notion, Tencent Docs, Feishu, WeCom, Tencent Meeting, and DingTalk. Connect or authorize them on the Plugins page to use their services in chat. by @grllll

Improvements

  • The agent team toggle in the model menu opens a settings pane with the switch and its cost explanation. by @Yifffan
  • Fix subagent model selection and message-source identification, apply authorization settings promptly, and improve result reporting and notifications of key findings. by @Dudu-0223
  • Improve reading and creating subagent and branched sessions, with a divider between inherited history and new messages. by @turtle2099, @Yifffan, @imccyu
  • Session lists and @-reference candidates load faster. by @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • Improve fuzzy and cross-language matching in search lists (for example, cpt finds /compact), and add name and path search to the workspace picker for new sessions. by @turtle1999, @acehinnnqru
  • Improve automation task recommendations, deletion dialogs, and session indicators, and add a Weekly review recommendation. by @ZiyaZhang, @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • DeepSeek image uploads run concurrently and retry when rate limited. by @CreatixChu
  • web_fetch shows readable causes and suggested actions for network failures. by @turtle2099, @Elevator14B
  • Improve code review with compact, collapsible change request lists, more timely GitHub PR status updates, and clearer diffs. Worktrees support custom branch names and remote base branches and are created after the first message is sent. by @LegGasai, @imccyu, @turtle2099
  • Make streaming output, turn navigation, and sidebar resizing more responsive in long conversations, and reduce stalls from large Markdown tables and lists in chat and document previews. by @turtle2099, @imccyu, @std-microblock
  • Improve interface display and list interactions with consistent icons, alignment, and spacing, improved sidebar scrolling and drag-and-drop ordering, and fixes for display issues. by @turtle2099, @grllll, @yixiangihsiang, @shigma, @std4453, @oraluben, @winewill, @07akioni
  • The /office command now covers Word, PowerPoint, and Excel. Migrate old format-specific commands and custom settings. Office guidance also explains how to work with existing PDFs using pypdf, included with Python. by @yudshj
  • Preview manual or automatic compaction summaries in the right sidebar from the compaction entry in the conversation. by @yudshj
  • Open Settings from the desktop application menu and see its current shortcut when it uses a single key combination. by @lsdsjy
  • Press ↑ in an empty composer to recall sent text from this conversation, then use ↑ or ↓ to browse it without overwriting a draft you are editing. by @std4453
  • Add “Add to chat” to file context menus to insert a reference and keep editing without sending the message. by @Kaige-Gao
  • Drag another regular session from the list into the right sidebar to continue chatting without leaving the current session. by @turtle2099, @Yifffan, @liukx0205
  • Customize reasoning levels for built-in catalog models in Model settings, or turn customization off to use the catalog defaults. by @LegGasai
  • Improve shared sessions across features, so viewers can take part in more of the session. by @mektpoy

Bug Fixes

  • Fix sessions getting stuck after switching models when the first request overflows the context; recovery and /compact summarize with the model that last answered. by @LegGasai
  • Fix repeated long waits when the DeepSeek image upload service is unresponsive and prevent cancelled uploads from shutting down the runtime. by @CreatixChu, @LegGasai
  • Fix restoring older sessions in single-file runtimes such as the Python wheel. by @Magolor
  • Pull requests touched by gh or git push inside PTC programs now appear in the code review panel. by @LegGasai
  • On macOS, Home and End in the composer move to the start or end of the line instead of scrolling. by @ZiyaZhang
  • Fix copy buttons doing nothing when the browser refuses clipboard writes. by @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • Fix input freezes and an unresponsive close button when dragging the Windows installer at completion, and fix leftover system registrations and unreported cleanup failures after uninstalling. by @winewill
  • Fix Open in app failing to launch VS Code, Cursor, and other Electron editors on Windows desktop. by @turtle2099, @huoyaoyuan, @turtle1999
  • Fix Cmd+W not closing the desktop window while the usage or top-up page is open. by @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • Fix schedule clocks appearing on sessions that only created or edited a task. by @Magolor
  • Fix draft attachments being lost when switching sessions; uploads and image previews are retained. by @mektpoy
  • Correct plugin management restart guidance: changes already in effect do not require a restart. by @turtle2099, @turtle1999
  • Fix nonexistent @ paths being displayed as file references. by @LegGasai
  • Fix experimental personalized pages failing to be created in the background. by @Dudu-0223
  • Fix excessive memory retention after read and glob process large files or result sets. by @turtle2099, @imccyu
  • Keep newly created file references and tabs pointing to the same files when a session changes directories; plugins that expect relative addresses from fileAddressFor must adapt. by @LegGasai
  • Clarify why team task claims fail and stop reporting write-scope overlaps for completed or deleted tasks. by @Dudu-0223
  • Fix generic Web app install icons appearing square by adding transparent rounded corners. by @EricMoin
  • Fix missing entries in desktop diagnostic operation history when distinct log files are mistaken for duplicates or Windows temporarily cannot open a file. by @Chinesezjc
  • Fix characters getting out of order or the cursor moving to the wrong position when typing an @ path. by @LegGasai
  • Resize oversized images to fit dimension and byte limits before sending them to models connected through pi, without changing the original attachments. See the upgrade guide for custom image settings. by @CreatixChu
  • Fix the Web app becoming inaccessible when old sign-in state accumulates across different ports on the same host. by @LegGasai
  • Fix the model picker closing unexpectedly when you click a group heading or blank space. by @shigma

Chores

  • Update system instructions incrementally by source and rebuild them after compaction. SDK user prompts accept a per-message time zone and fall back to the runtime time zone when available; custom integrations must follow the upgrade guide. by @Tuyixiang, @yudshj
  • Sync Cordis Core to 4.0.0-rc.12 and update Loader and Include; adjust related APIs, YAML parsing, and configuration validation, requiring plugins and custom configurations to follow the upgrade guide. by @shigma
  • Consolidate Creative mode's Cordis inspection tools into cordis_inspect, enable it by default in Standard mode, and support querying the running DSH version. by @turtle1999, @turtle2099, @Dudu-0223
  • Support Cordis plugin configuration on the frontend as well as the backend, with live synchronization for volatile fields. by @imccyu
  • With HMR enabled, edits to --patch overlay files apply without a restart. by @shigma
  • Reorganize plugin settings and enable/disable controls. Open plugin settings directly from the Plugins page; the Built-in plugins entry in Settings now opens plugin management. by @turtle2099, @LegGasai, @turtle1999
  • Computer Use unifies approval for system keyboard and mouse input on macOS, adds experimental Windows support, and improves reliability and setup guidance. by @huoyaoyuan, @agicy, @CreatixChu, @Dudu-0223, @kermanx
  • Computer Use can be served to external harnesses as an MCP server through cua-mcp, and cua_run_code is available by default in Standard mode and in SDK, headless, and similar integrations. by @LegGasai, @turtle1999
  • Node PTC now retains variables across calls by default and allows redeclaring them. Use inspect to display text, images, and tool results; oversized output is truncated, and Python PTC is no longer supported. by @kermanx, @imccyu, @turtle1999, @turtle2099, @Dudu-0223, @sjw1231, @tianyicui
  • Tool failures carry structured error names and codes; some existing codes are renamed (see the upgrade guide). by @sjw1231
  • Standardize module export entries: remove test-only exports and obsolete subpaths; import types from /types or the package root. by @imccyu
  • Session status moves to dsh-client-ui-session-status; plugins reading it must add it to inject. by @imccyu
  • Adjust the client plugin bundle layout: the framework installs stylesheets at mount time, and CSS Modules class names change format. by @imccyu
  • Keep file views and workspace-write scope aligned with session cwd; Git writes in worktrees require additional authorization. Remove working_directory and delegation cwd parameters, switch worktrees to enter_worktree / exit_worktree, and restore old sessions in their original project. by @LegGasai
  • The personalized recommendations plugin is enabled by default, and recommendations appear directly in the agent’s replies. by @ZiyaZhang, @Dudu-0223
  • Restore enable/disable controls and access to details for the experimental persistent terminal plugin; it remains disabled by default after installation. by @LegGasai
  • Adjust session storage with an experimental SQLite backend that requires manual configuration; JSONL remains the default. by @liukx0205
  • The API-key provider is now labeled DeepSeek API to distinguish it from the DeepSeek account provider. Model, search, and web-fetch requests use ctx.deepseekAuth for authorization; custom compositions and related plugins must follow the upgrade guide. by @turtle2099, @LegGasai, @liyao, @yudshj
  • Adjust branched-session creation APIs: custom fork calls must supply both the full history and parentSource. by @liukx0205
  • Add optional read-only tools for listing workspaces and sessions. Web also offers an opt-in tool for agents to send text on request to other regular sessions in the same runtime, including across workspaces. by @Dudu-0223
  • SDK session resume accepts a permissionMode that overrides stored permissions. by @koalazf99
  • Adjust list_agents to report occupied slots, the concurrency limit, and subagent tree members; callers using structured results must now read members from agents. by @Dudu-0223
  • Plugins can provide session-specific inline hints in an empty composer without modifying the draft. by @EricMoin
  • Add an optional on-demand MCP tool loading mode to reduce context usage in model requests. by @grllll

v1.0.3 Stable2026-11-22T04:03:41Z

Antigravity Google login, scriptable API-key setup, and always-on Jarvis voice

Highlights

  • Google personal accounts now sign in through Antigravity with Gemini 4 (Neon) as the default model and pooled Gemini, Argon, and Claude quota bars in the account router.
  • Every API-key provider now supports non-interactive login with --api-key, --api-key-env, and --json, including scripted AWS Bedrock and Azure OpenAI setup.
  • Always-on Jarvis voice now streams live captions as you speak, recovers automatically after network outages, and keeps a session-only replay history of accepted requests.

Improvements

  • Model and fuzzy pickers now rank literal substring matches ahead of typo and usage-boosted loose matches.
  • Todo lists now support withdrawing tasks by name with archived reasons while keeping retired and delegated work out of active reminders.
  • Queued follow-up messages now preserve image attachments and are delivered before retrying empty post-tool answers.
  • Long-running background tasks now trigger a one-time review after five minutes, while routine swarm wait receipts stay hidden from the transcript.
  • macOS menu bar session counts now render with native contrast, including 0/0 when idle.

Fixes

  • Reading a 13th screenshot no longer drops earlier images or invalidates the prompt cache just because of image count (retains tool images up to 48 MiB).
  • Retired Gemini Code Assist personal OAuth entries are hidden from login pickers and routed to Antigravity instead.
  • Jarvis speech playback waits until the microphone is quiet and keeps live captions, provisional turns, and final transcripts ordered per utterance.
  • Coordinator interrupts in swarms now stay scoped to the addressed session rather than interrupting unrelated workers.

Full changelog: https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/compare/v1.0.1...v1.0.3

This patch release improves vision model guidance by requiring reliable viewport evidence for CSS advice, ensuring more accurate styling recommendations for responsive components.

Improved

  • REQUIRE RELIABLE VIEWPORT EVIDENCE FOR CSS ADVICE (#4123): Vision model styling recommendations now require reliable viewport evidence, preventing CSS advice on components when viewport context cannot be verified. This ensures recommendations reflect the actual rendering environment and reduces false-positive style suggestions.

This patch release ships part A of exit-block improvements, which suggests appropriate reattach commands when session owners are still alive, plus a regression fix ensuring resume hints are preserved for completed launches.

Fixed

  • EXIT-BLOCK REATTACH SUGGESTION FOR ACTIVE SESSION OWNERS (#4117 Part A): Exit-block now suggests tmux attach or psmux attach when a detached session owner is still alive, reducing the need for manual session recovery. Part B (alternative attach paths) remains open. Fixes #4117 (partial).
  • PRESERVE RESUME HINT FOR COMPLETED FOREGROUND LAUNCHES (#4120): Fixed a regression from #4118 where the omx resume hint was being dropped for completed foreground launches. Resume hints are now correctly preserved throughout the launch lifecycle. Fixes #4119.

JuliusBrussee/caveman ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v4.2.0Caveman 4.2.0 Stable2026-11-22T07:10:51Z

Caveman 4.2.0 adds a QuickJS Code-Mode MCP gateway (caveman codemode / caveman wrap --codemode) with multi-agent overlays, multi-harness tool-envelope unwrapping in the compression engine, cache-safe proxy optimizations (local turn-effort gating, duplicate-read collapsing, canonical tool ordering, and defer_loading), @caveman-ai/middleware/codemode, opt-in Code-Mode support in @caveman-ai/pi 0.4.0, and a native Codex CLI provider for caveman-compress.

New

QuickJS Code-Mode MCP gateway (#1559)

  • caveman-mcp codemode and caveman codemode collapse large upstream stdio and HTTP MCP catalogs into a static, prefix-cache-safe set of Code-Mode tools (codemode_search_tools, codemode_execute, codemode_mutate) backed by an embedded pure-Go QuickJS sandbox (modernc.org/quickjs) plus built-in repo and PR helpers (batch_read, symbol_outline, pr_read, ci_logs).
  • caveman wrap --codemode installs temporary per-session Code-Mode overlays for Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Gemini CLI, Qwen Code, Kilo, OpenClaw, Pi, and Hermes (including config.yaml sync-back of in-session Hermes edits; Cursor MCP configs are discovered by caveman codemode).
  • Workspace MCP discovery is explicit: workspace-local MCP definitions are loaded only when --trust-workspace-mcp or CAVEMAN_TRUST_WORKSPACE_MCP=1 is set.
  • Exact recovery and non-text fidelity: Code-Mode execution outputs and built-in tools store originals in CCR before elision; upstream HTTP and stdio MCP decoding preserves integers above 2^53 and non-text attachment blocks (images, audio, resources), streams HTTP SSE replies incrementally, and strips auth/custom headers across cross-origin redirects.

Cache-safe proxy optimizations (#1559)

  • --turn-effort applies cache-safe per-turn effort gating across Anthropic (including Opus 4.7 / adaptive reasoning models), OpenAI/Codex, and Gemini without breaking prefix caches, replaying Codex/ChatGPT effort marks across substantive turns.
  • Duplicate-read collapsing, canonical tools[] ordering (--canonical-tools), MCP tool deferral (--defer-mcp-tools), and tool catalog shrinking (--shrink-tools) keep provider prompt prefixes byte-stable while trimming redundant input context. MCP-disabled streaming OpenAI Responses requests remain byte-identical.

Multi-harness engine envelopes (#1559)

  • The compression engine unwraps tool-result envelopes emitted by Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, and Hermes so code, search results, and test reports compress cleanly across harnesses while preserving Go build and setup failure diagnostics in verbose go test output.

Framework middleware and Pi (#1559)

  • @caveman-ai/middleware 1.2.0 adds @caveman-ai/middleware/codemode (createCodeModeToolset), a zero-dependency in-process Code-Mode toolset with BM25 discovery, structured pipelines, and read/write separation.
  • @caveman-ai/pi 0.4.0 adds opt-in Code-Mode tools (CAVEMAN_PI_CODEMODE=1) backed by a lazy CodemodeClient, honors CAVEMAN_MCP_PREFIX_ARGS, and extends RPC timeouts past caller script deadlines.

caveman-compress

  • Native Codex CLI provider for caveman-compress, isolated from local tools (#1427 by @danez).

Fixed

  • Engine and proxy: guard unwrapFileWrapper against whitespace-only input; recover per segment so an engine panic fails open while still re-raising http.ErrAbortHandler (#1518).
  • OpenCode: preserve whitespace in configured OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR (#1518).
  • CLI: remove stale dev and deploy aws commands that referenced removed targets.
  • Python SDK: restore the TLS byte budget after HTTP CONNECT reads (#1502).

Maintenance

  • Go toolchain bumped to Go 1.26.11 (#1532).
  • Documentation consolidated around AGENTS.md and docs/WHY.md, with public benchmark sample sizes stated alongside published figures.
  • Binaries: bin-v4.2.0. CLI: @caveman-ai/cli 4.2.0. Middleware: @caveman-ai/middleware 1.2.0. Pi: @caveman-ai/pi 0.4.0.

View release →

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-22T05:17:14Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2162+gbd54353246
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1795119671

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

browser-use/browser-use ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
1.0.8browser-use 1.0.8 Stable2026-11-22T03:30:41Z

pip install 'browser-use==1.0.8'

  • The judge asks as one more turn of the run, so the provider reads the run from its cache instead of billing the trace again. It returns the same verdict fields, gotchas included (#6457).
  • No API key over SSH or on a headless machine: the run prints a link and a code to approve on any device, then continues (#6427).
  • DeepSeek and Qwen in thinking mode: when the model refuses the forced final call at the step limit, the agent asks again without forcing, so the run returns its answer (#6453).
  • bu.type never prints a password field's value (#6455).
  • Browser(cdp_url=...) attaches the one page already open instead of a new blank tab (#6456).
  • Browser Use gateway calls reserve credit for 16,384 output tokens instead of 32,768, so small balances are not refused mid-run (#6461).

PRs: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/pull/6489, https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/pull/6504

View release →

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.81 Stable2026-11-21T21:53:04Z

Changed

  • The programming skill's language gate now routes rendered web work (.html, .css, .jsx, .tsx, .vue, .svelte, .astro, or any task that ships a UI) to the frontend skill before the first line of UI code. It tells the agent to take the look from the frontend skill's references when there is no design direction (no named brand, reference, DESIGN.md, or existing styled UI to match), and to finish only after visual-qa passes on the rendered surface. (#14604, fixes #14602)

Fixed

  • Memory sync: a sign-out now retires the trust pin of the account it signs out of, so omo account show --account <id> no longer answers joined once that sign-in's keys are gone. Plain omo account forget (the desktop's sign-out of a CLI-item session) used to retire nothing. It now reads the stored sign-in first to find its account. A stored sign-in that cannot be read is still deleted and its pin is left; one warning line is printed only after the delete succeeds. A forget that fails on a locked store reports only that error (#12308, #12384). (#13642) (#14533)
  • Memory sync: some sign-ins earned their pin before omo sync recorded their account. On a refused refresh, plain forget, forget --device and adopt --replace, omo now asks the service for that account, as omo logout already did, and retires the pin when the service names it. It asks only while a live pin exists and the access token has not expired; on a refused refresh, the token may still be valid in the 30 s before expiry, when the refresh runs. An expired token is never sent. A pin whose account stays unknown is kept, and omo prints one line naming omo account retire-sync --account <id> to clear it, because omo account show keeps answering joined for it (#12383, #13662). (#13642) (#14533)

Added

  • omo sync status (and --json) shows this device's memory sync state: the signed-in account, whether this device has joined and whether its sync switch is on, the account's devices with their sync membership and last-seen time, pending join requests, and whether an engine is running on this machine. It writes no sync state, prints no key material, and each section reports its own failure instead of failing the command. (#14437, fixes #14435) (#14533)

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.115 Stable2026-11-21T21:27:32Z

Security fix for a forced-SMB-authentication issue on Windows (GHSA-g6c9-j4vm-mwq4), plus a batch of extractor, resolution and incremental-update fixes. The extractor fixes that change output share one AST cache schema bump (50 → 51), so the first build after upgrading re-extracts the affected languages.

Security

  • extract/detect: indexing a repository no longer stats a corpus-chosen path that resolves outside the scan root. A .sh source/script-invocation line or a .git file naming a UNC gitdir:/commondir could coerce outbound SMB authentication on Windows (leaking a NetNTLMv2 response) on default offline settings. Probes are now bounded to the scan root and UNC paths are refused before they are resolved (GHSA-g6c9-j4vm-mwq4, thanks @Torch4084).

Fixes

  • path: an exact node id resolves before tier matching, so an id is no longer refused as ambiguous (#5367, thanks @Arav-Arun).
  • python: a cross-file call through an explicit from ... import ... is constrained to the imported name and module (#5229, thanks @Arav-Arun).
  • rust: colliding impl-block members (fn foo/fn _foo) each get a distinct salted node id (#5380, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • csharp: a call through a static member of a type (Audio.Instance.Click()) resolves through the member's declared type (#5376, thanks @ArnabOPP).
  • php: member calls resolve in remapped files and through the inherits/trait chain, keeping every call site (#5362, thanks @livingforelvis).
  • watch: a hook rebuild reconciles corpus-only changes (destination-only rename, ignore-file edit) so the graph no longer goes stale (#5331, thanks @sdivyanshu90).
  • update: an oversized JSON file is reported as skipped so a no-change update short-circuits at the early gate (#5131, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).

View release →

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v16.0.3 Stable2026-11-21T20:42:59Z

Critical fix

  • The OMP hook works with the server runtime (#6367, thanks @flipcyde101, closes #6366 reported by @walynde). When CLAUDE_MEM_RUNTIME was server or server-beta, the OMP hook still sent everything to the local worker, so OMP sessions were dropped. The hook now reads CLAUDE_MEM_SERVER_URL, CLAUDE_MEM_SERVER_API_KEY and CLAUDE_MEM_SERVER_PROJECT_ID (or the CLAUDE_MEM_SERVER_BETA_* names; environment variables win over settings.json) and talks to the server's /v1 API directly: session start, tool and reply events, context, and session end. It applies the same rules the worker applies before it saves anything: <private> blocks are stripped, excluded projects and CLAUDE_MEM_SKIP_TOOLS are honored, every prompt is recorded, and the project name comes from git. If the server settings are incomplete, it falls back to the worker as before.
  • OMP users: run npx claude-mem install --ide omp once to get this fix. The OMP hook is a copied file, so a plugin update alone does not replace it.

Fixes

  • Knowledge queries and corpus requests can no longer hang forever (#6368, thanks @flipcyde101, closes #6364 reported by @soarkit). A stalled Agent SDK stream used to keep the request and its Claude CLI child alive with no end. A knowledge query or prime now stops after 10 minutes and a corpus request after 15, with a 504 error. The corpus stays locked until the timed-out work has really stopped, so it cannot write after a delete or rebuild.
  • The "paused saving memories" notice goes away once Codex is saving (#6374, thanks @ryrenz, closes #6372 reported by @raw34). After you switched CLAUDE_MEM_PROVIDER to codex following a Claude quota pause, every new session still said capture was paused until the old Claude cooldown ran out. The notice is now recomputed when Codex stores a memory.
  • server api-key create --scope with no value is refused (#6375, thanks @ryrenz, closes #6373 reported by @soarkit). It used to create a key scoped to the literal string true, which every request then rejected. A bare --scope, --name, --team or --project now prints --<flag> requires a value and exits 1 without writing anything, on both create and migrate-scopes.
  • The Agent Cost Report no longer crashes on observations with no model (#6376, thanks @ZedingZhang, closes #6371 reported by @Realauster). acr.py rollup raised a TypeError when one window had both a missing model and a named, unpriced one.
  • The Docker server image builds again (#6386, closes #6379 reported by @InfoALR). It now uses node:20-trixie, because tree-sitter 0.26 needs a newer glibc than node:20 ships.
  • My Work refresh runs on OpenCode 2.x (#6361, refs #6339 reported by @Realauster). OpenCode 2.x rejects the --pure and --dir flags, so every refresh step exited 1. The step now checks opencode --version and uses each version's own isolation. 2.0.0 to 2.2.2 and 3.x are refused with a reason, because they cannot keep plugins out. On every version the refresh agent now has all tools denied.
  • OMP and Hermes read a worker port saved as a number (#6363, thanks @chuenchen309). {"CLAUDE_MEM_WORKER_PORT": 45678} in settings.json used to be ignored, so their requests went to the default port.
  • The OpenClaw installer no longer deletes a folder it did not install (#6381, thanks @DeryFerd). It ran rm -rf on whatever install path ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json named. It now replaces a folder only when it is missing, empty, or holds claude-mem's own OpenClaw manifest. Anything else is left alone with a warning and the default extensions folder is used; if that is also taken by something else, the installer stops.
  • The worker can stop every Claude child it starts (#6385, thanks @DeryFerd). Field compression and the Telegram wrap-up started their Claude process outside the worker's process list, so a worker that exited hard left them running. They are now on that list, and they do not take a concurrency slot.
  • A CLI stuck printing can't grow the worker's memory without limit (#6384, thanks @DeryFerd). Output collected from claude, codex, gemini and opencode is capped at 128 Mi characters, about 2.5 times the largest real turn measured. Past the cap, the start and end are kept, a warning is logged, and the reply is refused instead of read as a partial answer. Smaller outputs are unchanged.
  • A transcript record with no end can't grow the worker's memory without limit (#6383, thanks @DeryFerd). A JSONL record over 64 MiB (the largest real record measured was 44 MiB) is skipped, with a warning that names the file, the record's offset and its size. The lines after it are read as normal.
  • The unauthenticated /v1/info no longer shows Redis error text (#6380, thanks @DeryFerd). A queue lane that could not be read used to publish the driver's error, such as a host and port. The reason now stays in the server log.

New

  • Opt-in CPU cap for chroma-mcp (#6369, thanks @flipcyde101, partly addresses #6365 reported by @FengJie2004). Set CLAUDE_MEM_CHROMA_MAX_THREADS to 2 if chroma-mcp keeps several cores busy. It caps the embedder's threads and, on Linux with taskset, the CPUs chroma-mcp can use. It is off by default (0), because an always-on cap slowed the first backfill. #6365 stays open.
  • AIGROW preset for the OpenAI-compatible provider (#6370, thanks @guglielmo-io).

Hosted sync service

  • The sync services compare their internal projector credential in constant time (#6382, thanks @DeryFerd). A plain string compare could leak the shared secret through response timing. This changes services/sync-api and services/sync-hub, which are not part of the npm package: it reaches users only once those services are deployed, not through this release.

Tests

  • A flaky transcript test now counts the append poll's pokes instead of racing its file check (#6360).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v16.0.2...v16.0.3

CloakHQ/CloakBrowser ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
chromium-v156.0.8078.47.8-proChromium v156.0.8078.47.8 — Stealth Build (Pro) Stable2026-11-21T18:24:58Z

CloakBrowser Pro: Stable advances to Chromium 156.0.8078.47.8 (Linux) and 156.0.8078.47.7 (Windows)

Chromium 156.0.8078.47.8 is now Stable on Linux (x64 and ARM64), and 156.0.8078.47.7 on Windows (x64). macOS Stable stays on 156.0.8078.47.1.

With a valid CloakBrowser key, the wrapper automatically selects the Stable build for your platform. No version pin is required.

What's new since 156.0.8078.47.1

  • Challenge pages stuck on "Verifying…" pass more reliably. On sync Python, update the wrapper to 0.7.3 too.
  • Canvas tuned for Chromium 156. Chrome 156 changed how canvas renders, and our results now match real 156 machines, including on Linux servers without a GPU.
  • Image editing and upload pages work as in stock Chrome.
  • More consistent personas. Every place a site reads your persona from now agrees, at any zoom, including on ARM servers.
  • More stock Chrome 156 behavior restored, like payments, built-in AI, page unload, and browser error messages. The Windows persona also plays protected video, like Chrome on Windows 11.
  • Closer to Google Chrome on Google services with --fingerprint-allow-3p-cookies.
  • Better fonts for non-Latin languages on the Windows and macOS personas.
  • WebRTC calls and peer connections connect as in stock Chrome.
  • Windows open where you ask. Popups opened at a position, moveTo(), and --window-size windows report their real position, and on Linux the window fills the screen's usable area.
  • More realistic performance. Page timing follows stock Chrome more closely (Linux).
  • Several languages in one locale: --fingerprint-locale=en-US,el-GR gives you English pages while the browser still shows a second language.
  • Transparent proxy mode is steadier: fewer license hiccups, with backup DNS servers.

Your profiles, cookies, and seeds are untouched. Only the browser itself is replaced.

Current wrapper: v0.7.3

Update the wrapper before launching:

pip install -U cloakbrowser
npm update cloakbrowser
dotnet add package CloakBrowser --version 0.7.3

Roll back if needed

If a workload regresses, pin the previous Stable build:

export CLOAKBROWSER_VERSION=156.0.8078.47.1   # Linux, Windows

Remove the pin to return to the newest Stable build.

If you hit a regression, please tell us. Open an issue or email support@cloakbrowser.dev with the site and what you saw. Pinning the old build gets you unblocked; a report gets it fixed for everyone.

Checksums (SHA-256)

version=156.0.8078.47.8

ade92ae9254320687b7361044612bf11612425265d5166f51cea8c78b238d94f  cloakbrowser-linux-arm64.tar.gz

version=156.0.8078.47.7

The signed manifests are attached (SHA256SUMS + SHA256SUMS.sig for 47.8, SHA256SUMS-156.0.8078.47.7 + .sig for 47.7). The wrapper verifies the Ed25519 signature and each archive's SHA-256 checksum automatically.

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.269 Stable2026-11-21T17:57:28Z

Following 1.4.268, here is the next round of Orca. Bots learn eight more agents, and the phone app catches up with the desktop.

Bots on eight more agents, a fuller phone & a quieter Mac ✨

Bots can now run on Kiro, Kimi Code, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Kilo Code, MiMo Code, Droid and Goose, Chat opens more agents directly, and the phone app can answer, approve and set up almost everything the desktop can.

  • 🤖 Run bots on more agents: Bots can run on Kiro, Kimi Code, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Kilo Code, MiMo Code, Droid and Goose. Chat can open Kiro, Auggie and Goose too, and Kiro chats run your own hooks.
  • 📱 Do more from your phone: Answer an agent's questions and Google or Composio setup cards from its chat, review tool approvals and access requests in Needs you, manage an agent's tools and webhooks, start a new chat from +, and browse a group's threads and items. These need the matching mobile build.
  • 🔐 Antigravity, your way: Google account users can pick Orca's built-in Google sign-in or the agy agent, and agy has its own Sign in in Settings.
  • 💤 A quieter Mac when idle: Idle Mac terminals use less CPU, and Git work starts on the background process worker on macOS and Linux.
  • 🧭 Clearer chats: Older messages show calendar dates, Resume stands out in a paused queue, and Claude chats that settings block from Full access say so and pick the closest allowed mode.

About Orca Bots (Beta) <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="36" height="36" valign="middle" />

Orca Bots work like first-class teammates: they now run on many more agents, hand work to each other in group rooms, and send results you can actually open.

  • <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="28" height="28" valign="middle" /> Eight more agents behind your bots: Kiro, Kimi Code, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Kilo Code, MiMo Code, Droid and Goose join the agents a bot can run on.
  • Links show progress while Bots publish them, and you can reject an access request right from its Give access dialog.
  • 🤝 Hand-offs in group rooms: When a bot names another member as @Name in its reply, that member picks up the turn.
  • 📎 Results you can open: Bots share a link, attach the file or give the content instead of a path on their own computer, and local paths in Slack messages become readable labels.
  • 🧩 One skills folder per bot: A bot's own skills live in one agent-neutral folder that every agent it runs on can use.

Coming soon

  • Antigravity bug fixes
  • More connectors
  • Easier moving of bots between different machines
  • More performance improvements
  • Fix bots stall issues under heavy task load
  • Improved PR review workflow
  • More plugin API
  • Sidebar UX improvements

What's Changed

Bots can run on Kiro, Kimi Code, Auggie, Gemini CLI, Kilo Code, MiMo Code, Droid and Goose, hand turns to each other with @Name in group rooms, and share results as links or attachments. Bot Settings rows fit on one line, connector icons match, and access requests can be rejected from their confirm.
